Number Formatting, Multipliers & European/German Localizations" detailing `T` = Tausend = 1,000 (NOT trillion), comma decimals (`67,4T` -> 67,400, `24,98T` -> 24,980), dot thousands (`5.663` -> 5,663), and descending leaderboard coherence; added `raw_score` to the Gemini response schema to capture the exact on-screen string.
  - **Fixed (runtime sanitation & runaway inflation guard)**: implemented `parseOcrScore(rawScore, rawText)` and `normalizeDecimalString` in both the Edge Function and client runtime (`src/modules/ocr/ocr.service.ts`, `src/modules/app/ocr/ocr.controller.ts`, `src/core/gm-bridge.ts`, and `src/main.ts`), sanitizing incoming scores and re-scaling any numbers erroneously inflated into trillions/billions above FGF's 9,999,999,999 ceiling.
  - **Verification**: `npm run type-check` — 0 errors; **1356/1356 Vitest tests green across 114 suites** (+1 new suite / +13 regression tests in `tests/ocr_scores.test.js`); production build clean.

- **Added: GvG & SvS 9-tier daily chest thresholds and collapsible admin accordion (V3.18.1)** — updated in-game chest progression and enhanced admin ergonomics.
  - **Added (scoring & data model)**: populated canonical 9-tier score thresholds for each day of GvG and SvS in `src/core/scoring/chests.ts` (`GVG_CHEST_THRESHOLDS`, `SVS_CHEST_THRESHOLDS`); Day 1, Day 2..4, Day 5, Day 6 (PvP), and cumulative Day 0 (aggregate prep total) match official in-game requirements; `defaultChestGrid(eventName)` dynamically supplies the 63-tier grid (9 tiers * 7 days) corresponding to the chosen war event; `supabase/migrations/20260915120000_widen_chest_tiers_limit.sql` widens `gm_save_chest_tiers` array size constraint from 60 to 120 to support the complete 63-tier grid without truncation.
  - **Added (admin UI & UX)**: `#chest-tiers-card` in `index.html` is now wrapped in a semantic `<details class="gm-card gm-card-padded gm-section chest-tiers-accordion">` that is **closed by default**, eliminating vertical clutter on the Guild Admin dashboard while remaining immediately accessible with a single click and an animated chevron indicator.
  - **Verification**: `npm run type-check` — 0 errors; **1343/1343 Vitest tests green across 113 suites** (+1 new suite / +15 regression and accuracy tests in `tests/chest_tiers_defaults.test.js`); production build clean.

- **Fix: Discord webhook proxy 403 resolution and event-reminders category whitelist (V3.18.1)** — resolved end-to-end Discord dispatch failures across frontend announcements and automated cron reminders.
  - **Fixed (frontend 403 forbidden_client_webhook_url)**: `src/core/integrations/discord.ts` and `src/modules/members/members.service.ts` no longer supply `webhookUrl` in the payload to `discord-webhook-proxy`, complying with the Ticket 1.2 SSRF/open-relay prevention policy (`rawClientWebhook` 403 on client-supplied URLs); the Edge Function now securely resolves the webhook URL server-side from `guild_config` for the authenticated caller's guild; `allowEveryoneMention: true` is transmitted so `@everyone` mentions are preserved; direct proxy fallback now includes the user's `Authorization: Bearer <session_token>` header.
  - **Fixed (automated GvG, SvS, and Calamity reminders)**: added `'webhook_gvg'`, `'webhook_svs'`, and `'webhook_calamity'` to `STATIC_CONFIG_KEYS` in `supabase/functions/event-reminders/index.ts` so per-category webhooks are loaded from `guild_config` instead of returning empty strings and silently skipping 100% of GvG, SvS, and Calamity cron notifications.
  - **Verification**: `npm run type-check` — 0 errors; **1328/1328 Vitest tests green across 112 suites** (+5 new regression tests in `tests/discord_notification_dispatch.test.js`); production build clean.

- **Added: aggregate "Day 1 to 5" war score container (day 0), prep entry mode switcher and hybrid daily OCR pipeline (V3.18.1)** — shipped on 2026-09-13 (migration `20260915100000_war_aggregate_day0.sql` + redeploy of `member-portal` and `ocr-guild-members`).
  - **Added (data model)**: `event_daily_scores.day` and `event_chest_tiers.day` accept **0..6** — day 0 now holds the cumulative "Day 1 to 5 total" prep score while days 1..6 keep the per-day grid; `gm_save_chest_tiers` accepts the day-0 bucket (dedicated `v_last_agg` accumulator); `gm_upsert_daily_scores` re-defined with a **transactional mutual-exclusion rule** — zero double-counting, aggregate wins: a batch writing day 0 for a player deletes its Day 1..5 rows (and its own day 1..5 rows are skipped), a batch writing any Day 1..5 deletes its day-0 row, Day 6 is never touched; the `event_participants` cache stays derived (`score_prep = SUM(day IN (0..5))`, `score_pvp = day 6`, `score = NULL`); aggregate chests use the frozen day-0 tier slice with a deterministic fallback to the day-1 slice; member-portal rejects direct `score_prep` writes against sessions owning a `war_sessions` row (`granular_session_use_daily`).
  - **Added (TS mirror)**: `WAR_AGGREGATE_DAY` / `PREP_DAYS` constants and the canonical pure helpers `prepTotal()`, `aggregateTierSlice()` and `resolvePrepChests()` in `src/core/scoring/chests.ts` (strict SQL↔TS parity); `defaultChestGrid()` gains a cumulative day-0 block; `events.service.upsertDailyScores` typed for day 0..
  - **Added (UI)**: SvS/GvG **mode switcher** (Detailed D1–D5 vs Cumulative D1–D5) segmented control in the war banner, persisted per event in `guild_config` (`war_prep_mode:SvS|GvG`); aggregate grid = single "Total D1–D5" input (day 0) + Day 6 + chests; totals computed via `prepTotal(player) + day6` (no double counting); destructive conversion (total over existing Day 1..5) confirmed through the Dialog component — cancel reverts the inputs without writing, confirm saves and toasts `war_toast_total_replaced_days`; reverse conversion (a day over a total) saves directly with the informative `war_toast_days_replaced_total` toast; History session modal renders aggregate players as a "Total D1–D5" span cell with `resolvePrepChests` chests; Chest Tiers settings gain the day-0 "Day 1–5 (Total)" block; 7 new `war_*` i18n keys (golden 552 → 559).
  - **Added (OCR)**: the legacy aggregate OCR is fully **re-activated** (the `syncWarOcrGate` disable is removed everywhere — buttons are always clickable) and routed to the day-0 container (`svs_prep`/`gvg_prep` → day 0); the new **daily OCR** (`svs_day`/`gvg_day`) adds a Day 1..5 selector in the OCR modal feeding `metadata.scanDay`, validated by the Edge MetadataSchema as a strict Zod integer enum (1..6 — never interpolated from raw text) and substituted into the day-metric prompt; all SvS/GvG commits flow through `gm_upsert_daily_scores` (svs_prep/gvg_prep → day 0, svs_day/gvg_day → scanDay, svs_pvp/gvg_pvp → day 6, flushed ≤100 rows/call) — no direct `event_participants.score_prep` write remains; DTR stays on the legacy commit path.
  - **Verification**: `npm run type-check` — 0 errors; **1323/1323 Vitest tests green across 111 suites** (+44 vs the 1279/108 baseline: 23 Lot-1 day-0/SQL/TS-mirror tests, 9 Lot-2 UI switcher/confirmation tests, 12 Lot-3 OCR routing/scanDay/gate-removal tests, gate tests rewritten for the always-clickable buttons); production build stable (`version.json` V3.18.1). **Deploy note**: `supabase db push` (migration 20260915100000) plus redeploy of `member-portal` and `ocr-guild-members` before/alongside the frontend deploy.

- **Added: shadcn-like design-system architecture — cva/cn primitives, semantic theme layer, modern form controls, sticky war tables and modal dialogs (V3.18.1)** — the full P1→P3 UI/UX modernization cycle shipped on 2026-09-13 (frontend only: no SQL migration, no Edge contract change).
  - **Added**: canonical `cn()` (clsx + tailwind-merge, last-wins conflict resolution) and typed `cva`/`VariantProps` primitives (`src/core/utils/cn.ts`, `cva.ts`) plus the modular `src/components/ui/` library — Switch, Checkbox, Input, Badge, Table, Dialog, Tooltip and shared type enums — with **`clsx`, `tailwind-merge`, `class-variance-authority` as the only new runtime dependencies (no Tailwind/PostCSS/Vite; `scripts/build.js` untouched)**. New root **`theme.css`** semantic token layer aliases the untouched M3 base 1:1 (`--background`, `--foreground`, `--card`, `--popover`, `--primary`, `--ring`, `--radius-*`, …), dark-only, served right after `tokens.css` (root-level requirement enforced by the esbuild build loop).
  - **Added (UI)**: accessible toggle switches / checkboxes / form inputs — native inputs keep the legacy `id`s Guild Admin persistence reads, `role="switch"` + `aria-checked`, 44 px hit-slop, `var(--ring)` focus ring, cva accent/size variants, inline `aria-invalid` error states; war **Badge** (7 tones incl. SvS/GvG/Glory, soft/solid/outline/pill, `renderWarStatusBadge` W/L/tie/pending) and **Table** (sticky headers, alignment, `role="status"` empty/loading states, conditional row highlighting); compact large-score formatting (`formatCompactNumber` — K/M/B down-rounded up to 9,999,999,999) with exact-value `title` tooltips; accessible modal **Dialog** (ARIA `role="dialog"`/`aria-modal`, bidirectional Tab focus trap, Escape/backdrop/header-X close, `onConfirm`/`onCancel`/`onClose`, tone default/danger, size sm/md/lg/full, dedicated footer zone) and a declarative **Tooltip** singleton (mouse + keyboard triggers, `data-tooltip` init, `aria-describedby`).
  - **Changed**: Guild Settings form retrofitted onto the `gm-ui-*` primitives with the 18 `notify-*` toggles and 22 coeff/webhook/role fields fully id-stable; SvS/GvG W/L banner toggles and chest totals consume Badge, D1-D6 grids adopt the sticky `gm-ui-table-*` shell; History session modal consolidated onto Dialog (keeping the `history-modal*` surface and its delete-in-footer safety contract); OCR granular-gated buttons decorated with the tooltip (native `title` kept).
  - **Verification**: `npm run type-check` — 0 errors; **1279/1279 Vitest tests green across 108 suites** (+80 vs the 1199/104 baseline: 13 primitives/tokens, +20 P1 form components & Admin retrofit, +26 P2 Badge/Table & granular view, +21 P3 Dialog/Tooltip & History/OCR retrofits); production build stable (`version.json` V3.18.1), `dist/theme.css` shipped.

- **Fix: Discord reminder reliability — no more phantom or missing notifications (V3.18.1)** — shipped on 2026-09-13 (migration `20260914110000_fix_event_reminders_trigger_and_tz.sql`).
  - **Fixed (phantom reminders that kept firing daily while unchecked in Guild Admin)**: every notification toggle in `event-reminders` now evaluates through a single strict `isToggleEnabled()` rule (only the literal `'true'`/`'1'` enables; a missing/empty/`'false'`/`'0'` row now means **disabled**) — the old `config[key] === undefined || === 'true'` tests defaulted missing rows to ON, so a Guild Admin save that silently failed (RLS / expired subscription / offline DB) left the checkbox unchecked in the UI while the server kept sending; `setGuildConfig` now mirrors to localStorage only after a successful upsert, and the Guild Admin submit surfaces every failed write with an explicit error toast (`toast_config_save_failed`) instead of a fake success; the global `gvg_svs_calamity_enabled` gate was removed so only the event toggles decide; clearing a category webhook (e.g. `webhook_gvg`) now **silences** that category instead of falling back to `discord_webhook_url` (fallback applies only when the specific key was never configured).
  - **Fixed (scheduled reminders that never fired)**: `gm_invoke_event_reminders()` now evaluates its weekly slot table via `timezone('utc', now())`, so the cron guard can never be skewed by the database session timezone (the old `extract(dow/hour/minute FROM now())` silently dropped GvG/SvS/Calamity sends on non-UTC sessions), with an explicit `RAISE WARNING` when `event_reminders_cron_secret` is absent from Vault; SvS session detection is aligned on the current UTC calendar week (active `event_status.start_at` in-week, or a durable `war_sessions` SvS row of the week) instead of the fragile 7.5-day sliding window that killed reminders mid-cycle; `portal_5`/`portal_open` reminders are detached from `notify_svs_won_prep` (gated by `notify_svs_pvp` only, `won_prep` stays a content switch); standard-event matching is canonicalized (`normalizeEventName`/`isStandardEventName`/`eventPrefixForName` — trim + case-insensitive) so a slightly different label no longer disables the 30m/5m/start reminders.
  - **Verification**: `npm run type-check` — 0 errors; **1199/1199 Vitest tests green across 104 suites** (+16 P0 strict-toggle/webhook tests, +21 P1 trigger/timezone/SvS-week/name tests, including an SQL↔Edge schedule-coherence guard and a TZ-independence check on the new migration); production build stable (`version.json` V3.18.1). **Deploy note**: `supabase db push` (migration 20260914110000) plus redeploy of the `event-reminders` Edge Function.

## [3.18.0]

- **Raise max event score & glory tracking to 9,999,999,999 (V3.18.0)** — shipped on 2026-09-13 (migration `20260914100000_raise_event_score_limit_to_9999999999.sql`).
  - **Changed**: `MAX_ALLOWED_EVENT_SCORE` raised from 500M to **9,999,999,999** (single source of truth in `_shared/player-actions.ts`, propagated to the member-portal / Discord bot Zod schemas and `parseSafeScore`); score columns widened from INT4 to **BIGINT** (`event_participants.score/score_prep/score_pvp`, `event_daily_scores.score`, `weekly_scores.glory_score`) so values between 2.147B and 9.99B no longer overflow; `gm_upsert_daily_scores` re-defined with the raised per-day bound and BIGINT-safe derived sums; `gm_upsert_player_glory` re-defined with a `bigint` parameter and the new cap (old `integer` overloads dropped) so Glory scores up to 9,999,999,999 persist without the previous INT4 write-cast.
  - **Verification**: `npm run type-check` — 0 errors; **1162/1162 Vitest tests green across 102 suites** (+1 suite / +10 tests: static score-limit guards, Zod acceptance 500M→9,999,999,999 / rejection 10B, granular EventsView large-score forwarding); production build stable (`version.json` V3.18.0). **Deploy note**: `supabase db push` (migration 20260914100000) plus redeploy of `member-portal` / `discord-bot-interactions` / `ocr-guild-members` to propagate the shared constant.

- **SvS / GvG Granular War Management (V3.18.0)** — per-day (Day 1-6) scores with parametrizable chest tiers, explicit Won/Lost results and per-session opponent tracking for SvS & GvG, shipped on 2026-09-13 (migration `20260914090000_war_daily_scores_and_chest_tiers.sql`).
  - **Added**: four tenanted tables with forced RLS (`war_opponents`, `war_sessions`, `event_daily_scores`, `event_chest_tiers`); nine war RPCs (`gm_get_chest_tiers`, `gm_save_chest_tiers`, `gm_upsert_war_session`, `gm_get_war_session`, `gm_list_war_sessions`, `gm_upsert_daily_scores`, `gm_get_daily_scores`, `gm_delete_war_session`, `gm_list_opponent_candidates`) plus the deterministic chest scorer `gm_chests_for_score`; Guild Settings "SvS/GvG — Chest Tiers" card (SvS/GvG tabs, add/edit/remove a threshold per day, atomic save); SvS/GvG event banner with an opponent picker (server numbers / guild names, existing or created on the fly) and interactive Won/Lost toggles for Day 1-6; `granular` flag on `gm_list_event_sessions`; typed RPC service wrappers and the `resolveChestsForScore` TS mirror (`src/core/scoring/chests.ts`).
  - **Changed**: the SvS/GvG participant table now renders six live per-day score columns (Day 1..Day 6) plus a per-player chests total computed on the client, saved in a 700 ms debounced batch through `gm_upsert_daily_scores` — for granular sessions, `event_participants.score_prep` (D1-5) and `score_pvp` (D6) become transactionally derived cache columns (`score = NULL`, `participated = 1` when any day > 0); History session cards show `vs <opponent> · wW/lL` and the session modal switches to the six-day grid for granular sessions.
  - **Fixed**: the legacy SvS/GvG OCR aggregate buttons (`svs_prep` / `svs_pvp` / `gvg_prep` / `gvg_pvp`) are now disabled with an explanatory tooltip while a granular session is active, preventing aggregate writes from diverging from the day grid.
  - **Verification**: `npm run type-check` — 0 errors; **1152/1152 Vitest tests green across 101 suites** (+7 vs the previous 1145/101 baseline: chest-tier scoring parity, granular EventsView RPC coverage, i18n golden update to 551 keys); production build stable (`version.json` V3.18.0). **Deploy note**: `supabase db push` must be run to apply the war migration before/alongside the frontend deploy.

## [3.17.2]

- **Global remediation — Phases 1-3 (DB/RPC resilience, strict role isolation, frontend resilience & roster performance) (V3.17.2)** — three audit phases shipped together on 2026-09-13.
  - **Added**: `src/core/api/fetch-errors.ts` (unified network-error taxonomy: offline / gateway / rate-limit / unauthorized / server / unknown, classified toasts + inline Retry banner); `public.gm_check_ip_throttle` atomic throttle RPC (`SUM` + `INSERT … ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE`, `service_role` only); `accounts.session_version` epoch + `public.gm_session_is_valid()`; session-invalidation / error-classification test suites (`tests/fetch_errors.test.js`, `tests/roles_rls_session_hardening.test.js`).
  - **Changed**: tab router wired to `AbortController`/`AbortSignal` (aborts the previous in-flight batch on tab switch, dedupes redundant activations); strict role normalization in `_shared/admin-authz.ts` and `_shared/shadow-session.ts` — legacy `R5`/`R4`/`admin` values normalize to `member` and are forbidden by the new `accounts_role_check`; `gm_check_login` bounded `lock_timeout`/`statement_timeout` + atomic failure counter; `gm_invoke_event_reminders` due-window guard (active events in `[now-5min, now+35min]` or a live weekly GvG/SvS/Calamity slot) with `login-throttle-retention` pg_cron job; `player_absences` RLS self-read keyed on the in-game UID through `public.accounts`.
  - **Fixed**: peak-hour login/connection timeouts and Postgres lock contention (targeted indexes, strict timeouts, atomic throttle, retention moved off the request path); R5/R4 → `super_admin` role-escalation paths (exact helpers, `guild_admin` no longer elevated, direct PostgREST writes to `public.accounts` closed); `player_absences` policy comparing the wrong column (`app_metadata.account_id` vs in-game `uid`); duplicate-key storms on `guild_config` `sent_*` locks (`ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` via `acquireReminderLock`).
  - **Performance**: roster DOM event delegation bound once; `calculateRallyScore` memoized per sort pass; Tactical Force Matrix ranks in a single sort; O(1) precomputed `overall_power` max; cold-start `aria-busy` skeletons without clearing cached data on refresh.
  - **Security**: strict four-role isolation (`super_admin` / `server_admin` / `guild_admin` / `member`) with the legacy in-game roles retired; compromised GoTrue sessions invalidated via the `session_version` epoch bump on password reset / role change / suspension.
  - **Verification**: `npm run type-check` — 0 errors; **1133/1133 Vitest tests green across 100 suites** (+40 tests vs the previous 1093/97 baseline); production build stable (`version.json` V3.17.2), Service Worker compiled.


---

> ### Everything below predates the `## [x.y.z]` convention
>
> Releases **3.17.2 through 3.20.2 are documented above**, in order. The sections that follow are the historical log from before versioned headings were adopted: they are grouped by `## New` / `## Fixed` rather than by release, and each entry carries its version inline (e.g. `(V3.16.1)`).
>
> They have deliberately **not** been retro-assigned `## [x.y.z]` headings. A single section below spans several versions, and the mapping cannot be recovered reliably from the text — inventing one would put fabricated release history into the file this project uses as its memory. Read them as a dated engineering log, not as releases.

## New

- **Fix: Discord webhook notification role suppression, Shadowfront Squad 1 & 2 roles, and stale event auto-expiration (V3.16.1)** — Discord API suppresses push/bell notifications for role mentions (`<@&role_id>`) from webhooks unless `allowed_mentions: { parse: ['roles', 'users', 'everyone'] }` is explicitly provided. Added `allowed_mentions` to all 5 event reminder payload builders in `event-reminders`, defaulted it in `discord-webhook-proxy`, and enforced it across `gm-utils.js` and `shadowfront.js`. Added separate Discord role ID support for Shadowfront Squad 1 (`discord_role_id_shadowfront_squad1`) and Squad 2 (`discord_role_id_shadowfront_squad2`) with fallback to the global role in `event-reminders`, `gm-utils.js`, `shadowfront.js`, `index.html`, and `app.js`. Replaced hardcoded `@everyone` in SvS garrison reminder with `${svsGuildTag || '@everyone'}`. Added 24-hour auto-expiration in `event-reminders` so abandoned sessions never remain permanently active in `event_status`. Reconciled ALPHA's production state for tonight's Squad 2 session (`SF2-20260904-1` at 23:00 UTC). **712/712 Vitest unit tests green** (+8 new regression tests).

- **Fix: duplicate Discord notifications, and Web Push silently failing 100% of the time** — reported live: users got the GvG "Day 3" daily-task notification 6 times in a row. Root cause: `event-reminders`' per-guild "lock" (meant to guarantee a reminder is only sent once) used `.upsert()` against `guild_config`, which never errors on a primary-key conflict — it just silently overwrites. Under load, a slow invocation can outlast its own 1-minute cron tick and overlap with the next one; each overlapping invocation reads a stale "not yet locked" snapshot and both send. Confirmed directly against production: the 6 duplicate messages lined up exactly with 6 consecutive cron ticks (00:01-00:06 UTC) that all timed out waiting on that same slow invocation. Fixed by switching all 5 lock-acquisition sites (standard events, GvG daily tasks, GvG Saturday, SvS, Calamity) from `.upsert()` to a real `.insert()`, which correctly raises a conflict error against `guild_config`'s actual `(guild, key)` primary key — the existing "already locked, skip" handling then works as originally intended, regardless of how much invocations overlap. Separately found while investigating: Web Push (browser) notifications have been failing for **every single subscription, 100% of the time** — `sendNotification` (the `web-push-neo` library) takes 3 positional arguments, but the code called it with one wrapped object, so the library validated the wrapper instead of the real subscription and rejected every single one with "You must pass in a subscription with at least an endpoint," even though all 10 stored subscriptions were perfectly valid. Fixed to call it with the correct 3-argument signature. **648/648 Vitest unit tests green** (+4 new regression tests locking in both fixes). Deployed via the Supabase CLI; confirmed live post-deploy (clean, non-timing-out cron ticks).

- **Discord bot — Phase 2 (`/power`, `/glory`, `/timezone`, `/challenges`, `/absence`)**: five more self-service commands, all resolving the caller's linked account the same way `/myinfo` does and all writing through the exact validated, audited business logic `member-portal` already used — extracted into a new `_shared/player-actions.ts` (`updatePlayerPower`, `updatePlayerGlory`, `updatePlayerTimezone`, `getPlayerChallenges`, `listPlayerAbsences`, `setPlayerAbsence`, `deletePlayerAbsence`, plus their shared helpers), not reimplemented — `member-portal` itself now just calls these instead of inlining the logic, same pattern Phase 1 established for `getPlayer`. `/power <valeur>` and `/glory <valeur>` update the same bounded fields (`MAX_ALLOWED_POWER`/`MAX_ALLOWED_EVENT_SCORE`) the Portal enforces. `/timezone <offset>` is bounded natively by Discord (`-12`..`14`), matching the RPC's own range. `/challenges` shows the same weekly-challenge/season-level breakdown as the Portal tile. `/absence` is Discord's first subcommand-group command here (`set`/`list`/`cancel`) — `set` fixes `kind` to `full` for this V1 (the `reduced` mode stays Portal-only), `list` shows each absence's id so `cancel` can reference it. **644/644 Vitest unit tests green** (+24 new: real logic tests against the actual exported `_shared/player-actions.ts` functions through a minimal fake Supabase client — bounds clamping, validation-error paths, RPC-failure fallback — plus static regressions confirming neither `member-portal` nor `discord-bot-interactions` duplicates this logic). `tsc --noEmit` clean, build clean. Deployed (`member-portal`, `discord-bot-interactions`) via the Supabase CLI.

- **Discord bot — Phase 1 (account linking, `/link`, `/myinfo`)**: the first piece of a slash-commands-only Discord integration (no Gateway connection — every command is a plain HTTPS interaction, verified by Ed25519 signature, matching the same "public endpoint + verified signature" pattern already used for the Stripe webhook). New `discord-bot-interactions` Edge Function; `/link` generates a short-lived code shown only to that Discord user, confirmed from the already-authenticated Player Portal (My Info → Link Discord) — the confirmation is what proves ownership, never the code by itself, since a UID or code alone is guessable/visible to guildmates. `/myinfo` reads the linked player's real stats (power, glory, role, fleet, timezone) via the exact same roster lookup `member-portal` already uses (now shared via `_shared/member-lookup.ts`, avoiding both duplication and a fake internal auth context). Two new tables (`discord_link_codes`, `discord_account_links`, 1:1 enforced at the DB level). **620/620 Vitest unit tests green** (+8 new, including real Ed25519 signature verification against generated test vectors), Ed25519 support on the Supabase Edge Runtime confirmed live (valid signature accepted, forged signature rejected) before the rest was built on top of it. Remaining before this is reachable from real Discord: the 3 app secrets need to be set in the Supabase dashboard, the Interactions Endpoint URL needs to be pasted into the Discord Developer Portal, and the two commands need to be registered via `scripts/register-discord-commands.mjs` — all three are manual, credential-bound steps outside what an assistant should do on someone's behalf.

- **Font-size consistency — Phase B (JS inline styles), migration complete**: the same mechanical migration from Phase A applied to the 461 inline `font-size` usages across the 13 root `.js` files that build their HTML by string concatenation (`app.js`: 155, `svs-matchup.js`/`gvg-matchup.js`: 72 each, `portal.js`: 34, `gm-leader-hall.js`: 30, `stats.js`: 26, and 8 more files). Every one of the ~100 distinct raw values found across the whole app (CSS and JS combined) is now one of the 12 `--fs-*` scale steps added in Phase A — no remaining ad-hoc `font-size` value anywhere in the app. `node --check` clean on all 13 files, **612/612 Vitest unit tests green**, live-verified the login page, sidebar, and OCR modal render identically. The one-off migration script (`scripts/migrate-font-sizes.mjs`) is removed now that both phases are done.

- **Font-size consistency — Phase A (type scale + CSS files)**: the app had no shared type scale — `tokens.css` already centralizes every color, but `font-size` was ~100 distinct near-duplicate values (e.g. `0.75rem` / `.75rem` / `0.74rem` / `0.76rem` / `0.78rem` all meaning "small text") scattered across ~150 declarations in the 4 stylesheets alone. Added a 12-step `--fs-2xs` through `--fs-6xl` scale to `tokens.css`, each step centered on the most frequent value already observed at that weight class (a consolidation of existing drift, not a redesign — net visual change is 1-2px at most per touched rule). Migrated all 411 static `font-size` declarations in `components.css`/`styles.css`/`shell.css` to the new variables via a one-off script (deliberately left untouched: the handful of `clamp()`-based responsive sidebar rules, which stay hand-tuned). **612/612 Vitest unit tests green** (unaffected, CSS-only), live-verified across the login page, the admin sidebar/dashboard, and the OCR modal with no regression. Phase B (the same migration across the 13 root `.js` files' inline styles, ~461 more usages) is next.

- **Fix: sidebar footer elements nearly touching on real desktop screens** — reported live. Measured directly: the subscription pill, "Buy Me a Coffee", "Report a bug", and the version/legal footer were separated by as little as 1-5px, with text as small as 9.28px, at completely ordinary viewport heights (650-720px, which most real desktop windows fall under via an existing `@media (max-height: 720px)` compact rule) — not an edge case. Root cause: the `clamp()` floors and the compact-mode overrides for that stack had simply been tuned too tight, likely from before "Buy Me a Coffee" was a permanently-visible item there. Raised every floor (font-size, padding, margin) in both the base rule and the compact override to a readable/breathable minimum, verified directly (gaps went from 1-5px to 5-10px, text from ~9-10px to ~11-12px) with no new overflow introduced — the nav list above already absorbs extra footer height via its own scroll area, confirmed live at 650px height. 612/612 Vitest unit tests green (unaffected, CSS-only), live-verified via direct DOM measurement before and after.

- **Fix (follow-up): `server_admin` still saw "Admins only" on the donation page itself** — reported live: the nav link was now visible (previous fix), but clicking it landed on an access-denied screen. Root cause was the exact same allowlist bug, independently duplicated: `subscription-view.ts` (the `/src` TypeScript port of the donation panel) has its own separate `isAdminRole()` check that never imported or reused `shell.js`'s logic, so fixing the nav didn't fix the page behind it. Same fix applied: exclude by role (`member` only) instead of allowlisting `super_admin`/`guild_admin`. New regression test executes the real module in jsdom and confirms `server_admin` sees the actual donation tiers, not the denial screen. **612/612 Vitest unit tests green** (+1 new), live-verified against the built bundle (confirmed both `server_admin` and the already-working `guild_admin` now behave identically at this gate).

- **Fix: `server_admin` couldn't see the "Buy Me a Coffee" link at all** — reported live via a user screenshot. `visibleNavItems()` gated every `adminOnly` sidebar entry (today, only the donate link) behind an allowlist of exactly `super_admin`/`guild_admin` — `server_admin`, a real, legitimate admin role, silently fell through. Fixed to exclude by role (`member` only) instead of allowlisting specific admin roles, so a future admin sub-role can't hit the same gap. Also gave the link a warm coffee-brown accent with a gentle steam-pulse animation on the icon (`prefers-reduced-motion`-aware) — applied consistently to the admin sidebar, the mobile drawer, *and* the Player Portal's own donation nav item (already visible to every player, no bug there, just added the same visual treatment for consistency). **611/611 Vitest unit tests green** (+9 new), live-verified the exact CSS renders (computed color, border, and animation all confirmed applied) against the built app.

- **New: detect a stale long-lived tab and prompt a refresh** — reported live: a user saw two different app versions depending on which guild tab they had open. Root cause: a tab that's already loaded never re-fetches its own JS on its own, so a tab left open (or a PWA install) since before a deploy silently keeps running old code indefinitely — not just an outdated version number, every bugfix shipped since is missing too. The build now writes a tiny `version.json` marker (single source of truth: read straight from `gm-utils.js`'s `APP_VERSION`); the client polls it (network-fresh, never cached) on tab-visibility regain and every 15 minutes, and shows a dismissible "Update available" banner with a Refresh button the moment it disagrees with what's actually running. **Vitest unit tests green** (+4 new), confirmed live: production `gm-utils.js` and `version.json` both correctly report the current version with proper `must-revalidate` caching (not the stale-CDN theory first suspected).

- **Self-service guild creation: "Clear History" for resolved requests** — reported live: the Guild Requests panel accumulates every request forever, approved/rejected included, with no way to tidy it up. New "Clear History" button next to Refresh removes every already-resolved (approved/rejected/expired) request in one click, behind a confirmation dialog; **pending requests are never touched** by this, enforced server-side (`clear-resolved-guild-requests` filters `status != 'pending'`, not client-side filtering the admin could bypass by tampering with the request). New regression test locks in both the super_admin gate and the pending-exclusion filter. Live-verified directly against production: inserted one pending and one resolved test row, ran the exact delete predicate, confirmed only the resolved row was removed. **602/602 Vitest unit tests green** (+2 new).

- **Self-service guild creation — Phase B (public form + admin review panel)**: completes the feature Phase A's backend laid the groundwork for. The login page's segmented switcher gains a third tab, **"Request a Guild"**, next to Sign In and Register Player — guild tag, server number, desired admin login, Discord handle, and an optional note, all client- and server-validated with specific error messages (tag/id already taken, too many pending requests, rate-limited, etc.) rather than a generic failure. Super Admin → Accounts & Access gains a **"Guild Requests"** card listing every submission with Approve/Reject actions; approving shows the freshly generated admin password once, inline, with a one-click copy button (auto-copied to the clipboard too) — the same "reveal once, relay via Discord" pattern every other admin-created account already uses. Rejecting immediately frees the tag and admin login for a future attempt. New static regression test locks in that the submitted Discord handle and note — the first fully anonymous, unauthenticated free-text fields ever rendered into a privileged admin view in this app — are always HTML-escaped, never interpolated raw. **600/600 Vitest unit tests green** (+4 new), **14/14 Playwright e2e tests green** (+4 new, covering the public form's tab switching, submission, and error states with a stubbed backend). Live-verified end-to-end against production: submitted a real request through the actual login page UI, confirmed the exact row landed in the database with the exact submitted values, then rejected it to free the tag back up.

- **Self-service guild creation — Phase A (backend foundation)**: anyone can now request a new guild without already being an admin, mirroring the existing player self-registration pattern one level up. New public, rate-limited Edge Function `guild-request` validates the request (guild tag format, 4-digit server number, desired admin login id, a Discord handle as the only contact channel since this app has no email system, an optional note) and inserts it as a `pending` row via a new `gm_submit_guild_request` RPC — never touching `guilds`/`accounts` directly. A `super_admin` reviews every request manually (`admin-accounts` gains `list-guild-requests`/`approve-guild-request`/`reject-guild-request`); approving atomically re-checks for tag/id collisions, creates the guild row, and provisions the first `guild_admin` account through the exact same GoTrue-shadow-user sequence the existing "Create Account" flow already uses — with one deliberate difference: the requester never chooses their own password (unlike a self-registering player, who is vouched for by their own guild's join code, a guild-creation requester is a total stranger asking for `guild_admin`, so the login password is generated server-side only at approval and shown once for the admin to relay via Discord). Two partial unique indexes close the race between two people requesting the same tag/id at once; pending requests older than 14 days auto-expire via a daily `pg_cron` job, and a 200-row global pending cap bounds a slow-drip spam script. Never payment-gated — this app is 100% free (see the `free_app_and_donations` migration). **596/596 Vitest unit tests green**, plus live verification against production: submitted a real request through the deployed Edge Function over HTTP, confirmed the DB rejects a duplicate tag/admin id, and confirmed reject correctly frees both for a future attempt.

- **Members OCR now also detects and assigns R1-R4 rank** — the roster scanner (screenshots or video, since it shares the same pipeline as the video feature above) now reads the rank section headers the in-game roster groups members under — "Executive Manager" (R4), "Rank 3 Members" (R3), "Rank 2 Members" (R2), "Rank 1 Members" (R1) — and pre-fills a detected rank for every existing and new member, shown as an editable dropdown right next to their extracted power so it can be reviewed or corrected before committing, exactly like every other extracted field already works. R5 (guild leader) is never touched — same protection the manual bulk rank-assignment buttons already enforce, reused as-is rather than reimplemented, so a scrolled roster can't accidentally reassign the leader. For a video recording specifically, a section header can scroll out of view partway through — the rank context is carried forward frame-to-frame (and across the batches the recording gets split into) so members still get the right rank even in frames where no header is visible. **589/589 Vitest unit tests green** (+1 new), plus a live round trip against production: a synthetic roster image with all four section headers was uploaded through the real OCR modal, and every player's rank, power, and username came back correctly matched to its section — R5 protection separately confirmed via the same authorization check the manual bulk-assignment buttons use.

- **OCR: submit a video instead of 25 screenshots** — the AI OCR scanner (Members, Power, Fleet, Glory, SvS/GvG, Shadowfront, DTR, Arms Race — all 17 metrics) now accepts a screen-recording video alongside screenshots, in the exact same drop zone. Instead of manually screenshotting every screen-full of a leaderboard, record one continuous scroll through it and drop the video in — the browser samples the recording and keeps only the frames where the visible content actually changed (a lightweight visual comparison against the last kept frame, not a fixed "every N seconds" guess), so a slow scroll doesn't waste the batch on near-duplicate frames and a fast scroll doesn't silently skip rows. Kept frames are capped at 25 and spread evenly across the whole recording — not just its first few seconds — then flow through the exact same batching and AI extraction screenshots already use. Screenshots remain fully supported and unchanged; this is an additional input, not a replacement. Videos over 4 minutes or in an unreadable format get a clear error instead of hanging. **588/588 Vitest unit tests green** (+6 new, covering the frame-selection and capping logic), plus a live round trip against production: a real recorded test video was uploaded through the actual OCR modal and correctly processed end-to-end (video decoded, frames extracted and sent, real AI response rendered) with no errors, twice — once with changing content and once with a static (unchanging) recording.

- **Fix: admin password login broke after heavy passkey testing, masked behind a wrong error message** — reported live: after signing into the Player Portal with a passkey a few times, admin sign-in (both password *and* passkey) started failing with "Invalid credentials." even though the password was correct and the account wasn't locked. Two compounding bugs:
  - The real cause was the IP rate limiter (`login_throttle`, 30 attempts / 5 minutes) — shared between `auth-login` and `auth-passkey` since both key it purely by IP, and a passkey sign-in costs 2 requests (options + verify) rather than 1, with the new conditional/autofill listener adding a background `auth-options` call on every page load. Heavy passkey testing from one IP was enough to exhaust the same budget password login draws from. Limit raised 30 → 60 per 5 minutes in both functions to give real mixed password+passkey usage headroom.
  - That exhaustion was invisible: the login form's error banner was mistargeting `loginError.querySelector('span')`, which matched the *icon* span (always empty), not the message span next to it — so no matter what error came back, the banner (when it showed at all) stayed stuck on the default "Invalid credentials." text. This bug predates passkeys entirely and affected every login error, not just rate-limiting. Fixed to `'span:last-of-type'` in both the login and registration forms, and explicit friendly messages added for `too_many_attempts` ("Too many attempts. Please wait a few minutes and try again.") and `account_locked`.
  - Separately, `supabase-js`'s `functions.invoke()` silently discards the real response body on any non-2xx HTTP status (`.data` comes back `null`) — so a 429 from the rate limiter was reaching the client as a generic, unlabeled failure regardless of the two fixes above. Added `parseFunctionsInvokeResult()` (recovers the actual JSON body from the error response) and wired it into every call site that talks to `auth-login`, `auth-passkey`, and `member-portal`, so a real server-provided error code now always reaches the UI.
  - **Verification**: reproduced the exact reported sequence live (Player Portal login → back to login → admin password login) against production before and after — confirmed broken, then confirmed fixed. Separately forced a real 429 by pre-filling the throttle table and confirmed the login form now shows the correct "Too many attempts" message instead of "Invalid credentials." **582/582 Vitest unit tests green**, no regressions.

- **Fix: picking a passkey from Proton Pass's suggestion list didn't sign in** — the conditional-UI fix above got the suggestion to appear, but selecting it silently failed to complete sign-in. Root cause: `verifyRegistrationResponse`/`verifyAuthenticationResponse` (the library backing passkey verification) default to *requiring* biometric/PIN user verification unless told otherwise — but this app only ever requests `userVerification: "preferred"` from the authenticator (never `"required"`), so the server was silently enforcing a stricter bar than it ever asked for. A password-manager extension that honors "preferred" by not re-verifying on a given ceremony (which can differ between a full sign-in flow and an autofill-triggered one) would then have its perfectly valid sign-in rejected. Both verification calls now explicitly match the "preferred" intent instead of silently upgrading it — user *presence* is still required either way, only the verification step was ever optional. **582/582 Vitest unit tests green** (+2 new), confirmed no regression to how garbage/forged credentials are still rejected.

- **Fix: passkeys saved in a password-manager extension (Proton Pass, Bitwarden, 1Password...) didn't show up at all** — the discoverable "Sign in with a Passkey" fix above still relies on the browser's own modal passkey picker, and third-party password-manager extensions have inconsistent support for that specific picker across browsers, even though the passkey is genuinely saved. Added a second, complementary flow: **conditional UI** (the same mechanism password autofill uses) — as soon as the login screen is shown, the app quietly arms a background listener, and any saved passkey matching the site (from Face ID/Touch ID/Windows Hello *or* an extension like Proton Pass) now appears as a suggestion the moment you focus the identifier field, with no button click needed. The explicit "Sign in with a Passkey" button stays as-is for browsers/situations where that's unavailable. Verified live: the browser call now correctly uses `mediation: "conditional"` with no restriction to one account, and starting a fresh listener correctly cancels a stale one (e.g. after logging in through another method) rather than leaving it running in the background.

- **Fix: "Sign in with a Passkey" only showed passkeys for one account at a time** — if you had passkeys registered on more than one account (e.g. a `super_admin` account and a separate Player Portal account), typing one account's identifier before clicking the passkey button only ever offered that account's own passkeys, since the ceremony was scoped to whatever id was typed. The identifier field is now optional for passkey sign-in: leave it blank and the browser's native passkey picker shows *every* passkey registered for the site across every account — the server figures out which account from the passkey you pick, not from anything typed beforehand. Typing an identifier first still works exactly as before, narrowing the picker to just that one account. No re-enrollment needed — this only changed how login looks passkeys up, not how they're stored. **580/580 Vitest unit tests green** (+3 new), full round trip re-verified live against production for both the scoped and the new discoverable path.

- **Passkeys / WebAuthn — Phase D (admin reset escape hatch)** — completes the Passkeys feature:
  - New `admin-reset-passkeys` action on `auth-passkey`: revokes every passkey, backup code, and pending challenge for a target account in one call, for the case where someone has lost every enrolled device *and* their backup codes. Authorization is the exact same `canManageAccount` matrix `admin-accounts`' existing "reset password" action already uses and that's already covered by its own tests — a `guild_admin` can only reach accounts in their own guild, a `server_admin` only their own server, nobody but `super_admin` can touch a `super_admin` account, and a `member` can't call this action at all. The account falls back to password-only; it isn't deleted or otherwise altered.
  - New **"Reset Passkeys"** button next to the existing password-reset button on every account card in Accounts & Access (admin accounts and player/member accounts both, since it's the same shared list-rendering code) — confirms first, since it's irreversible.
  - **Verification**: authorization matrix tested live end-to-end against production — missing target, nonexistent target, a `guild_admin` correctly blocked from resetting a real `super_admin` account's passkeys (confirmed that account's 2 real passkeys and 10 real backup codes were left untouched), a `member` blocked from calling the action at all, and the success path (`guild_admin` resetting a member in their own guild) both via direct API calls and by clicking through the real UI — confirm dialog, success toast, and the target's passkey actually gone from the database afterward. New regression test asserts the action sits behind the identity gate and calls the shared authorization matrix, not a bespoke/looser check. **577/577 Vitest unit tests green** (+3 new).

- **Passkeys / WebAuthn — Phase C (sign in with a passkey or a backup code)**:
  - `auth-passkey` gains three unauthenticated actions (part of the login flow itself, handled before the identity gate): `auth-options`/`auth-verify` (the passkey sign-in ceremony) and `backup-login` (redeem a one-time code). Same generic error for a nonexistent account and one with no passkeys, so the endpoint can't be used to check whether an account exists. A successful sign-in via either method resets the same lockout counters a correct password would.
  - **"Sign in with a Passkey"** button and a **"Use a backup code instead"** link added to the main login screen, next to the existing password form — both call through `gm-webauthn.js`'s new `authenticateWithPasskey()`/`redeemBackupCode()`, matching the exact JSON marshalling the registration ceremony already established. `window.GM.loginWithPasskey()`/`loginWithBackupCode()` in `gm-utils.js` apply the resulting session to the Supabase client the same way password login does, so every downstream success path (guild/server restriction lookup, dashboard vs. Player Portal routing) is unchanged and shared, not duplicated per login method.
  - New **"Secure your account"** prompt shown once per browser session after a plain password sign-in for an account with no passkey yet, using the `has_passkey` field `auth-login` already returns (added in Phase A) — dismissible, and a "Set up now" button that jumps straight to Accounts & Access or My Info → Security.
  - **Found while wiring this up**: the player-facing "Player Submission Portal" screen (`#portal-step-lookup`, a second, separate login form) is pre-existing dead code — the button that would reveal it (`go-to-portal-btn`) doesn't exist anywhere in `index.html`, so it can never actually be shown; real player logins go through the main login form instead, which already routes `member` accounts straight to the Player Portal. Passkey/backup-code buttons were still added there for consistency with its sibling password field, but this whole screen is a candidate for a separate cleanup pass.
  - **Also fixed**: `detectClientEnvironment()` (used for both bug-report tickets and default passkey device labels) was showing wrong OS/browser versions on Apple devices — confirmed live on a real iPhone (iOS 26.6.1 showing as "iOS 18.7") and a real Mac (macOS 26.6.2 showing as "macOS10"). Root cause: Safari and every other browser freeze the OS-version token in their user-agent string on both iOS and macOS as an anti-fingerprinting measure, so it no longer reflects the real installed version — the fix stops presenting that stale number as fact and just reports the platform name. Separately, Safari's own version was showing the full internal WebKit build number ("Safari 26.6.1") instead of just the major version every other browser branch already reduces to — fixed to show "Safari 26", matching Apple's current versioning where Safari's number tracks the OS's yearly release. Existing passkey device labels aren't retroactively renamed; rename them manually from the passkeys list if needed.
  - **Verification**: full real end-to-end login round trips executed live for both new methods against production — backup-code sign-in (generate real codes, redeem one through the actual UI modal, land on the dashboard with a real session, confirm single-use) and the passkey ceremony as far as this environment allows (challenge round-trip, clean error handling for no-passkey/garbage-credential/expired-challenge cases — a full hardware-authenticator round trip isn't testable here). Both the "secure your account" banner and its suppression after a passkey/backup-code login verified live. **574/574 Vitest unit tests green** (2 updated for the OS/browser detection fix).

- **Passkeys / WebAuthn — Phase B (enrollment UI, multi-device, backup codes)**:
  - `auth-passkey` extended with `list`/`rename`/`remove`/`regenerate-backup-codes` actions, all ownership-checked (`account_id` match, not just row id). `register-verify` now also generates a fresh set of 10 backup codes (format `XXXXX-XXXXX`, unambiguous alphabet, SHA-256 hashed at rest) automatically the first time an account enrolls a passkey — one shared set per account, returned in plaintext exactly once; a 2nd/3rd device doesn't get a new set. `regenerate-backup-codes` lets the user get a fresh set anytime, invalidating the old one.
  - New `gm-webauthn.js` — hand-rolled client-side WebAuthn marshalling (base64url ↔ ArrayBuffer conversion for the registration ceremony), deliberately without the `@simplewebauthn/browser` CDN dependency; the actual cryptographic verification stays server-side in the audited `@simplewebauthn/server` library. Also exports a shared `mountPasskeysPanel()` UI builder (list/rename/remove/backup-codes card) reused identically by both surfaces below, so that UI logic exists exactly once.
  - New **"My Passkeys"** card at the top of admin **Accounts & Access**, and a new **"Security — My Passkeys"** section in the player Portal's **My Info**: register a passkey per device (default label auto-detected from the browser/OS, renamable inline afterward), see when each was added/last used, remove one, and download/regenerate backup codes (shown once, with a one-click text-file download).
  - **Verification**: full backend lifecycle curl-tested (`list`/`rename`/`remove`/`regenerate-backup-codes`, ownership checks, backup-code hashing) against a manually-inserted test credential, cleaned up after. Client-side verified live: real challenge round-trip inspected mid-flight (correct `ArrayBuffer` decode of the server's base64url `challenge`/`user.id`), registration ceremony correctly reaches and reacts to `navigator.credentials.create()`, both the admin card and the portal card render and load real data end-to-end for `DemoAdmin` and `DemoPlayer` respectively. A full enrollment with a physical authenticator isn't testable in this environment (no virtual authenticator available) — the gap is server-side verification logic, already covered by Phase A's live tests. **574/574 Vitest unit tests green** (+1 new).

- **Passkeys / WebAuthn — Phase A (backend foundation)**:
  - New tables `webauthn_challenges`, `account_passkeys`, `account_backup_codes` (`supabase/migrations/20260901000000_webauthn_passkeys.sql`) — RLS enabled with zero policies (deny-all to `authenticated`/`anon`), same pattern as `login_throttle`; every read/write goes exclusively through the new Edge Function below, mirroring how the `member` role already has zero direct database access.
  - New `supabase/functions/auth-passkey/index.ts` Edge Function, following `member-portal`'s reference pattern (cryptographic JWT identity resolution, IP rate limiting, structured logging, global try/catch). Phase A scope: `register-options` (generates a WebAuthn registration challenge via `@simplewebauthn/server`, excludes already-registered credentials for the account, stores the challenge server-side) and `register-verify` (verifies the attestation, stores the new credential with a device label). `rpID`/`origin` pinned to the real production host (`guildmanagement.vercel.app`), so registration only works from production, not localhost or a preview deploy. Backup-code generation, passkey login, and the admin reset escape hatch are later phases of this same feature.
  - `auth-login`'s shadow-GoTrue-session bridge (deriving/creating the synthetic GoTrue user used to mint a real session after a real credential check) extracted into `_shared/shadow-session.ts` so the new Passkeys function can issue an identical session after passkey/backup-code verification instead of duplicating that security-sensitive logic. Behavior of `auth-login` itself is unchanged, plus one small addition: the login response now includes `has_passkey`, so the client can offer to enroll a passkey right after a successful password sign-in for accounts that don't have one yet.
  - Existing accounts are unaffected: password login keeps working exactly as before for every account, with or without a passkey.
  - **Verification**: `@simplewebauthn/server@10.0.1` confirmed working on Supabase's Deno runtime via a live spike deploy before building on it. Both Edge Functions redeployed and tested live end-to-end (real `DemoAdmin`/password login confirming zero regression and the new `has_passkey` field; a full `register-options` → challenge-persisted-in-DB round trip; and clean, non-500 error responses for unauthenticated requests, unknown actions, and a garbage credential). **573/573 Vitest unit tests green** (+5 new).

- **Admin Player History**: Stats leaderboard rows are now clickable and open a per-player history modal — a power/glory trend chart and a per-event-type attendance summary, for any player in the admin's scope. Built on `gm_get_player_metrics_history` (an RPC deployed and authorized for exactly this since 20260823, but never called from anywhere in the app until now) plus a new `gm_get_player_event_history` RPC (same "self OR in-scope admin" authorization model, query logic mirrors the player Portal's own `get-history` action) for the event side. The chart itself reuses the player Portal's own canvas renderer (extracted to `gm-utils.js` as `drawScoreTrendChart`/`formatChartDate`) instead of a third implementation.

- **Cross-Server Migration Groups**:
  - New feature: the game periodically opens a limited-time window grouping a cluster of servers together, letting players migrate to any guild within that cluster. Previously every transfer path (`request_guild_transfer`, `gm_transfer_guild_member`, `resolve_guild_transfer`, `gm_admin_request_transfer`) enforced a strict same-`server_number` rule with no way to relax it.
  - New tables `server_migration_groups` / `server_migration_group_servers` (`supabase/migrations/20260830072000_cross_server_migration_groups.sql`) and a `servers_in_open_migration_group()` helper. Only a `super_admin` can create/open/close a group (RLS: read gated on `gm_can_read_guilds()`, write gated on `is_super_admin()`); the linkage is a time-boxed window (`status: open|closed`), not a permanent list — closing it turns off eligibility immediately, including for a transfer request that was made while the group was open but hasn't been approved yet (re-validated at approval time, not just at request time).
  - New Super Admin UI card "Cross-Server Migration Groups" (next to Subscription Management & Server Numbers) to create a group (name + server numbers), and open/close existing ones.
  - New `gm_list_transfer_eligible_guilds(p_guild)` RPC — the single source of truth for "which guilds can this guild transfer players to" (same server, or same open migration group), used by both the admin "Transfer Member" dialog (cross-server options clearly labelled "— Migration Window") and the player portal's self-service transfer request picker, so neither UI can ever offer a target the backend would reject.
  - **Verification**: full lifecycle (no group → rejected as `different_server`; group opened → transfer succeeds and carries full history; group closed → rejected again, including a pending request that becomes unapprovable) verified live end-to-end against production with a real `guild_admin` account and isolated, cleaned-up test data. **520/520 Vitest unit tests green** (+11 new).

- **Bulk Member Rank Assignment & Leader Hall Display Standardization (V3.7.1)**:
  - **1-Click Multi-Member Rank Updates**: Added full multi-selection capability to the Members tab (`#admin-members`) in both **Roster Cards** and **Military Matrix** view modes with high-contrast glow states.
  - **Floating Bulk Action Dock**: Sleek Material Design 3 dock appearing smoothly on member selection with real-time selection counter, 1-click rank buttons (`🌱 R1`, `👤 R2`, `🛡️ R3`, `⚔️ R4`), and instant deselect controls.
  - **Toolbar Select All / Deselect All**: 1-click select all toggle in the members list header.
  - **Strict Guild Leader (R5) Protection**: R5 rank is strictly excluded from bulk target choices, R5 leader checkboxes are disabled, and filtering (`filterBulkEligibleMembers`) safeguards guild leadership from accidental batch modification.
  - **Single-Query Supabase Batch Persistence**: Executes bulk rank updates in a single database operation (`.update({ role: targetRole }).in('pseudo', eligiblePseudos)`), instantly refreshing state and recalculating groups.
  - **Leader Hall Player Display Standardization (`[Guild] Pseudo #Server`)**: Standardized all player displays across Leader Hall (message bubbles, quotes, sidebar, @mention popups, participant pickers, and treaties) to strictly use `[Guild] Pseudo #Server`, eliminating internal `guild_admin` role mentions.
  - **Clean Date & Time Meta (`DD/MM/AAAA HH:MM`)**: Formatted message timestamps with exact calendar date and hour:minute without noisy seconds, preserving microsecond-accurate monotonic sorting under the hood.
  - **High-Contrast `@player` Mentions**: Upgraded mention pills with dedicated contrast palettes across both dark (incoming) and blue (outgoing) bubbles with glowing `@Me` highlight badges.
  - **Floating Bottom-Edge Reaction Badges**: Positioned emoji reactions as floating chips overlapping the bottom border of message bubbles, eliminating bubble stretching and height distortion.
  - **Quality Gate**: **499/499 Vitest unit tests green** (+19 new unit & DOM tests across `tests/members_bulk_rank.test.js` and `tests/leader_hall_rich_chat.test.js`), 0 TypeScript errors, clean production build.

- **Guild Leader Hall, Diplomatic Treaties, Signal Protocol E2EE & Anti-Capture Security (V3.7)**:
  - **4-Section Accordion Command Architecture**: Left navigation organized into 4 collapsible spaces: `🌐 Global Leaders Lounge` (Global Lounge, Diplomacy & Treaties), `👤 1:1 Discussions`, `👥 Group Discussions`, and `👑 R5 Summit`.
  - **Dynamic Group Management & Participant Modals**: Live group renaming (header title editing + `gm_rename_e2ee_conversation` RPC) and participant manager dialog (`#modal-group-members` + `gm_add_e2ee_group_members`, `gm_remove_e2ee_group_member` RPCs) to dynamically add/remove leaders after creation.
  - **Inter-Guild Diplomatic Treaties Registry**: Official treaty drafting and bilateral ratification protocol in `# 📜 Diplomacy & Treaties`. Standardized auto-titling (`Treaty between [Guild A] ([Server A]) and [Guild B] ([Server B])`), 1-click N.A.P. clause generator, validity countdowns, and bilateral digital signature requirement to achieve public ratified status across the FGF galaxy.
  - **Leader Moderation & Discussion Controls**: Comprehensive controls to leave groups, delete discussions, and block/unblock leaders.
  - **End-to-End Encryption (Signal Protocol Engine)**: Implemented complete zero-knowledge cryptographic core (`gm-signal-crypto.js` + `src/core/crypto/signal.ts`) featuring **X25519** ECDH key agreement, **Ed25519 / Schnorr** signatures, **HKDF-SHA256**, and **AES-256-GCM** authenticated encryption.
  - **1:1 Direct Diplomacy**: Protected with **X3DH** (Extended Triple Diffie-Hellman) asynchronous key handshakes and the **Double Ratchet Algorithm** ensuring continuous forward secrecy and break-in recovery.
  - **Group Discussions & War Councils**: Multi-member group chat utilizing the **Signal Sender Keys Protocol** with forward secrecy on member eviction and key rotation.
  - **👑 R5-Only Summit Protection**: Guild Masters can create exclusive high-level summit groups restricted strictly to R5 leaders and Super Admins.
  - **Anti-Screen Capture & Anti-Copy Protection**: Multi-layered defense-in-depth security restricting text selection, disabling right-click context menu, real-time 30px privacy blur shield on window blur/multitasking/screen capture attempts, forensic background watermarking (`[GUILD] Account • Server • CONFIDENTIAL`), and print/PDF hiding.
  - **Cryptographic Safety Fingerprints**: 60-digit safety numbers comparison modal to defeat Man-in-the-Middle (MITM) attacks and guarantee authenticity.
  - **Monotonic Sequence Ordering & Exact Second Timestamps**: Messages sequenced strictly by integer database sequence ID and formatted down to the second (`HH:mm:ss`).
  - **Blind Server Relay & Zero-Trust RLS**: PostgreSQL schema (`20260825000001_guild_leader_hall_e2ee.sql`, `20260825152000_guild_leader_hall_actions.sql`, `20260825153000_diplomatic_treaties.sql`, `20260825161000_leader_hall_tenant_access_audit.sql`) acting as a blind ciphertext relay; strict RLS locking out the `member` role from all leader communications.
  - **Quality Gate**: **468/468 Vitest unit tests green**, 0 TypeScript errors, clean production build.

- **Comprehensive Feature & UX Major Update (V3.6)**:
  - **1-Click Quick-Clear Search Buttons**: Added circular "X" quick clear buttons across all search bars (Members list, Member Portal, Banned Players, Sanctions, OCR filter, SvS, GvG, Arms Race, Glory, Cross-Rank) with zero-overhead CSS and event delegation.
  - **Game UID Validity Qualification & Red Alerts**: Universal FGF in-game UID validation (`window.GM.getUidStatus(uid)`) verifying numeric 6-10 digit identifiers, and flagging temporary placeholders (`TEMP-*`, `TMP-*`), missing values, and malformed inputs with red warning badges platform-wide.
  - **Quick Action Header Counter Badge & Roster Filter**: Added `⚠️ X Temp UID` in the Members list header to filter incomplete UIDs in 1 click, alongside `⚠️ Temp / Missing UID` tier filter and `⚠️ Sort: Temp UID First` option.
  - **Member Edit Guidance**: Live guidance and border feedback when replacing temporary placeholder UIDs with real game UIDs in the member edit dialog.
  - **Shadowfront Squad Attendance Ratio Visibility**: Added historical Shadowfront attendance fraction (e.g. `4/4`, `3/4`, `0/4`, `-`) to member rows in **Main Participants** and **Substitutes** (`renderAssignedRow` in `shadowfront.js`), matching the Member Pool.
  - **Shadowfront Toolbar Responsive Alignment**: Re-architected `.sf-compose-bar` and `.sf-compose-controls` ensuring "Share on Discord" is strictly right-aligned across desktop, tablet, and mobile viewports.
  - **Quality Gate**: **460/460 Vitest unit tests green**, 0 TypeScript errors, clean build.

- **Bug-Reporting Ticketing System (V3.5.3)**:
  - **Report a Bug**: any `guild_admin`, `server_admin`, or `super_admin` can report a bug from the top-bar / desktop sidebar button.
  - **Structured Ticket Schema & Media Attachments**: dedicated modal form fields for Title, Current Behavior / Issue, Expected Behavior, Steps to Reproduce, Additional Context / Information, and Auto-detected Client Environment (FGF Version, OS + Version, Browser + Version).
  - **Screenshot & Media Uploads**: drag-and-drop, clipboard paste (`Cmd+V` / `Ctrl+V`), and file picker for up to 3 screenshots with client-side canvas compression (`compressImageForUpload`), thumbnail management, Discord embed image attachment, and Super Admin lightbox zoom.
  - **Global sequential ticket numbers** (`#TICKET-xxxx`): the `tickets` table uses a single global sequence (race-safe, cross-guild), with guild / reporter account / reporter role attribution.
  - **4-Stage Status Lifecycle & Discord Webhook Broadcast**: new tickets start with status `waiting` ("Waiting for resolution"). Super Admins can update ticket status between `waiting` (⏳ Waiting for resolution), `in_progress` (🔨 In progress / Working on it), `resolved` (✅ Resolved), and `closed` (🔒 Closed). Every status change immediately broadcasts a formatted embed update to the dedicated Discord status webhook.
  - **Tickets console (super admin)**: "Tickets" nav item lists every report from every guild (newest first) with 4-state status controls, structured formatted description layout, and interactive screenshot lightbox.
  - **Security**: reporter is resolved server-side from `accounts` (member + anonymous rejected); the `gm_create_ticket` RPC follows the service-safe `IF auth.uid() IS NOT NULL` guard so browser and Edge calls both work; audit trail via `gm_log_player_audit`.
  - **Quality Gate**: **447/447 Vitest unit tests green** (+status change and Discord notification test assertions), 0 TypeScript errors, clean build.

## Fixed

- **UX Audit Follow-Up, Phase D: Mobile Tables, Nav Reorganization, Button/Modal Unification — and a Critical `/src` Regression Caught Along the Way**:
  - **CRITICAL — `src/main.ts` was silently disabling the real SvS/GvG Matchup tool**: while verifying the responsive-table change below, live testing surfaced that `window.GM_SVS_MATCHUP`/`window.GM_GVG_MATCHUP` were a `class` (a `/src` port), not the expected object — `typeof` returned `"function"` instead of `"object"`. `src/main.ts` was bridging `SvSMatchupView`/`GvGMatchupView` (`svs-matchup.ts`/`gvg-matchup.ts`, 50 lines each: a single hardcoded RPC call to fixed server numbers `'1001'`/`'1002'`, no real table render even on success) over the top of `svs-matchup.js`/`gvg-matchup.js` (728/697 lines: side-by-side rosters, combined dangerosity leaderboard, sorting — the complete, working tool). Both write into the same `#svs-matchup-container`/`#gvg-matchup-container`; since `/src/main.ts` is a deferred module, it always ran last and always won. This has been live and completely hiding the real tool behind a non-functional stub since the build-pipeline fix earlier this session (`7a3b8da`) let `/src/main.ts` execute in production for the first time. Fixed by removing the two bridging lines — `GM_CROSSRANK` (`cross-rank.ts`, a substantially complete 431-line port) was left untouched.
  - **Responsive tables for SvS/GvG Matchup**: applied the `.gm-resp-table` + `data-label` pattern (already used by Members/SvS/GvG/DTR scoring/Stats leaderboard) to all 3 tables in each of `svs-matchup.js`/`gvg-matchup.js` — previously the only tables in the app still using plain horizontal scroll on mobile.
  - **Leader Hall mobile breakpoint aligned to 900px** (was 768px, `shell.js`'s own `BREAKPOINT_MOBILE`): both the CSS (`.gm-leader-hall-layout`, `.gm-chat-back-btn`) and two JS `window.innerWidth <= 768` checks controlling the mobile full-screen chat mode — closing the 769-900px seam where the shell considered the viewport mobile but Leader Hall didn't. The other 4 unrelated `768px` blocks in `styles.css` (input font-size, Members/Dashboard grids, search bar) were left untouched.
  - **Navigation reorganization**: Leader Hall moved from the `Admin` section to `Activity` (`play`) — it's a real-time, daily-use chat surface, not an occasional configuration screen. The Donate/"Buy Me a Coffee" link moved out of `Admin` entirely into a lightweight footer link next to the sidebar's legal footer, via a new `section: 'footer'` category.
  - **Unified the two parallel button/modal systems**: 10 dialogs (`showEditMemberDialog`, the generic `showConfirm`, the "UID already taken" dialog, `pickEventStart`, the History session detail modal, the OCR modal, the custom Discord message modal, and 2 more) were still using the legacy `.confirm-overlay`/`.confirm-card`/`.primary-btn`/`.btn-ghost`/`.btn-danger` classes instead of `.gm-modal-overlay`/`.gm-modal-card`/`.gm-btn`. Two of them (`showUidTakenDialog`, the OCR modal) were genuinely mismatched — a modern `.gm-modal-card` wrapped in a legacy `.confirm-overlay` parent, which silently broke their pop-in animation since neither CSS rule's selector chain matched. All converted to the single modern system; existing per-dialog width/alignment overrides preserved via inline styles.
  - **Dead CSS removed**: the unreachable `[data-theme="light"]` block (confirmed zero JS/HTML ever sets `data-theme`) and the `.leaderboard-table` family (`.leaderboard-wrap`, `.rank-cell`, `.week-cell`, `.score-badge` and variants, `.profile-btn`) — superseded by `.gm-table`/`.gm-resp-table`, confirmed zero remaining references.
  - **Verification**: live as `DemoAdmin` on the minified `dist/` build — sidebar section membership confirmed via DOM query, footer donate link and its click wiring exercised with `isPaymentsDisabled` temporarily stubbed, `showConfirm`/Edit Member dialogs confirmed rendering the unified classes with preserved sizing, the matchup-tool regression confirmed and re-verified fixed with `isSuperAdmin` temporarily stubbed (both tools render their `gm-resp-table` correctly; DEMO simply has no cross-server matchup data configured, an unrelated data-availability state, not a code issue). **568/568 Vitest unit tests green** (+17 new, including a dedicated regression guard for the `/src` shadowing bug).

- **UX Audit Follow-Up, Phase C: Reactivity — Parallel Queries, Batched Writes, TTL Cache, Minified Build**:
  - **Parallelized two independent queries** in `showTransferMemberDialog` (`gm_list_transfer_eligible_guilds` and the current guild's `server_number`, previously awaited one after another with no dependency between them) — same `Promise.all`-style pattern already used 18+ other places in the codebase.
  - **Batched 6 sequential per-row Supabase call sites** that used to `await` one player at a time: the 3 event-participation branches of `commitOcrUpdates()` (Shadowfront / Arms Race / SvS-GvG-DTR), its 2 roster-metrics branches (existing-member updates, new-member inserts), and the Events "Approve All" pending-submissions loop. A guild committing OCR results for 30-80 players previously made 30-160 sequential round trips; now they run in parallel batches of 10 via a new shared `window.GM.runInChunks()` helper (`gm-utils.js`) — chunked rather than one giant `Promise.all` to stay under Supabase's per-connection concurrency limits.
  - **TTL freshness cache on the Stats and History tab loads**: switching away and back within 15 seconds no longer re-fetches and re-renders from scratch — a small `window.GM.cache` module (`gm-utils.js`) tracks per-guild freshness in memory, invalidated explicitly the moment any mutation the Stats/History pages care about happens (member add/edit/delete, both OCR commit paths, both transfer paths), so a genuinely stale view is never shown, only a redundant one skipped. `history.js`'s `loadHistory()` also gained a re-entrancy guard it never had before (a rapid double-click could previously fire two overlapping fetches).
  - **Legacy JS/CSS files are minified at build time** instead of copied byte-for-byte: `scripts/build.js` now runs each of the 22 root `.js` files and 4 CSS files through `esbuild`'s `transform()` (already a project dependency, already used identically for `noble-crypto.bundle.js`) before writing to `dist/`. Roughly 1.65 MB → 933 KB (-44%) of JS/CSS shipped on every page load, with the existing `node --check` syntax gate still running against the un-minified source first.
  - **Verification**: live against production as `DemoAdmin` on the actual minified `dist/` build — confirmed byte-identical behavior to the unminified source (Player History chart pixel-for-pixel match, `debounce`/`runInChunks` unit-verified in-page), the TTL cache measured directly via an RPC call counter (0 extra `gm_stats_data`/`gm_list_event_sessions` calls on a quick tab revisit, exactly 1 after an explicit `invalidate()` simulating a mutation). **551/551 Vitest unit tests green** (+15 new).

- **UX Audit Follow-Up, Phase B: Statistics Granularity**:
  - **`gm_stats_data` promoted to canonical migrations and extended**: like 4 other event RPCs found and fixed earlier this session, `gm_stats_data` — arguably the most important RPC for the entire Stats page — existed only in the pre-consolidation migration archive, not in `supabase/migrations/`. Promoted, and extended its projection with `shadowfront_squads.squad`/`.session_id`/`.is_commander` and `event_participants.late`/`.excused`/`.appointed` — all already written by the admin UI, just previously dropped before reaching the Stats page.
  - **Shadowfront granularity**: Event Deep-Dive now shows Squad 1 vs Squad 2 assignment counts and a Commander badge per player, on top of the existing titulaire/réserve split shipped in Phase A.
  - **Late / Excused column**: added to Event Deep-Dive (Shadowfront, Arms Race, SvS/GvG, DTR) — data History already tracked per-session but Stats had no aggregated view of it. Deliberately not shown on the Leaderboard views (Global Score / Attendance Rate), which don't compute these fields and would otherwise show a permanently empty column.
  - **Glory added to Event Deep-Dive**: previously absent from the event picker entirely, even though its data (`raw.glory`) was already being fetched for the Global leaderboard's bonus calculation. Glory has a different shape than participant rows (no session concept, no prep/pvp split) so it gets its own normalization branch in `loadSingleEventMode()` rather than being forced through the participant-shaped aggregation path.
  - **Verification**: migration applied and queried live against production (`gm_get_player_event_history` exercised end-to-end for a real DEMO player, returning correct per-session score/guild-average/guild-max across all 7 event types present in the data); all Stats changes exercised live as `DemoAdmin` with real data (Shadowfront squad/commander breakdown, Arms Race counts, Glory tab, Player History modal chart + event list); the player Portal's own chart re-verified unaffected by the `drawChart()` extraction (3 canvases, all still rendering real content). **536/536 Vitest unit tests green** (+16 new).

- **UX Audit Follow-Up, Phase A: Navigation Bugs, Dead View, Onboarding Data Leak, Search Reactivity, Stats Display**:
  - **Notification click didn't navigate**: clicking a Leader Hall native/PWA notification from another tab called `window.GM.shell.gotoItem`, which never existed (`window.GM.shell` is never assigned — the real bridge is `window.GM_SHELL`). Fixed silently-failing navigation in `gm-leader-hall.js`.
  - **Menu label mismatches**: the "Accounts & access" sidebar item opened a page whose `<h1>` read "Command Center" (now "Accounts & Access", matching); the Super Admin menu had two entries with the literally identical text "Accounts & access" pointing at two different pages (now "Super Admin Accounts" for the second one).
  - **Removed the dead `#member-view` block** (176 lines, `index.html`) — confirmed unreachable: every reference to it only ever added the `hidden` class, nothing ever removed it. Discovered in the process that this also orphaned the entire "member variant" of `renderGuildMembers()` in `app.js` (its own search box, tier filter, sort, and roster list all lived exclusively inside that dead view) — removed that dead code path too rather than leave it half-alive.
  - **New-guild onboarding data leak**: a brand-new guild's Overview page silently saved 5 hardcoded real people's names (from the original developer's own guild) into its own Timezone Clocks config on first load, before any admin action. Now defaults to an empty state ("No timezone clocks yet") and only ever writes to config when an admin explicitly adds a clock.
  - **Member search had no debounce**: typing in the Members search box re-ran a full filter+sort+re-render (plus two unrelated subpanels) on every keystroke, with no delay — for a 50-150 member guild, a 5-character search fired 5 full render cycles back-to-back. Added a shared `window.GM.debounce()` utility (`gm-utils.js`), applied to the member and banned-player search boxes; filter/sort changes now also skip the two unrelated subpanel re-renders (absences, timezone coverage).
  - **Stats display gains**: Shadowfront's Event Deep-Dive now shows the titulaire/réserve split per player (the `role` field was already being fetched from the backend, just never read); Arms Race Stage A/B now shows the real attendance count instead of a plain checkmark (the count was already being computed, just collapsed to a boolean at render time).
  - **Background timers now respect the visibility API**: the three `setInterval` UI-refresh loops (sidebar/topbar countdown, Overview clock ticker, Overview countdown ticker) no longer do work while the tab is backgrounded.
  - **Verification**: live end-to-end against production as `DemoAdmin` (search debounce isolated with a synthetic call counter — 5 rapid calls collapse to 1 — plus real Shadowfront/Arms Race data confirming the new columns render correctly, zero console errors). **520/520 Vitest unit tests green.**

- **Event System Hardening: Shadowfront Crash, Dead Availability Subsystem, Leaderboard Coefficients, Missing Canonical RPCs**:
